'x5cidj2hy87s'\nnp.random.seed(sum(ord(c) for c in _zhang2025_seed) % 2**31)\n\n# the loader always pulls these four spike arrays\nimport brainbox.io.one as bio\nbio.SPIKES_ATTRIBUTES = ['clusters', 'times']\n\nfrom one.api import ONE, One\nfrom one.alf.exceptions import ALFWarning\nfrom brainbox.io.one import SessionLoader, SpikeSortingLoader\nfrom iblatlas.regions import BrainRegions\n\n# the trials table and the spike sorting are each listed at two revisions, and ONE warns\n# once per load that it is taking the later one, which is the one we want\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore', category=ALFWarning)\n\n# --- constants ---\n\nHERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))\n# Directory holding the one_cache/ folder. Harbor mounts the dataset at /app/data while\n# this script runs from /solution, so solve.sh overrides this with --datadir.\nDATA_DIR = os.path.join(HERE, 'data')\nRELEASE = 'Brainwidemap'\nONE_CACHE_DIRNAME = 'one_cache'\nBASE_URL = 'https://openalyx.internationalbrainlab.org'\nPASSWORD = 'international'\n# ONE expires a cached REST response five minutes after it is written. The cache shipped\n# with the dataset is pre-dated so it survives; this keeps what this run caches alive too.\nREST_EXPIRY = datetime.timedelta(days=365 * 50)\n\nT_START, T_STOP = -0.5, 1.5      # decoding window around stimulus onset, following the reference code\nBIN = 0.02\nN_BINS = int(round((T_STOP - T_START) / BIN))            # 100\nEDGES = T_START + BIN * np.arange(N_BINS + 1)\nTIME = EDGES[:-1] + BIN / 2                              # bin centres, the first decoder input\n\n# reaction time bounds of the paper, which truncates the distribution at 80 ms and 2 s;\nMIN_RT, MAX_RT = 0.08, 2.0\n\nQC_LABEL = 1.0                   # the \"stringent quality control\" of the paper, 75,708 units of 621,733\nSPLIT = [100 / 3, 2 * 100 / 3]       # equal sized classes, from percentiles of each session's own trace\n\nCHOICE = {1.0: 0, -1.0: 1}       # +1 is a leftward choice, -1 rightward; 0 is a no response, dropped\nPRIOR = {0.2: 0, 0.5: 1, 0.8: 2}\nMOVEMENT = ['low', 'medium', 'high']\n\nINPUT_NAMES = ['time_from_stimulus_onset', 'trial_number_in_block']\nOUTPUT_NAMES = ['choice', 'prior_probability_left', 'wheel_speed', 'whisker_motion_energy']\nOUTPUT_VALUES = [['left', 'right'], ['0.2', '0.5', '0.8'], MOVEMENT, MOVEMENT]\n\n# the cache location is not known until --datadir is parsed, and the connection cannot be\n# shared across a fork anyway, so connect() fills this in per process rather than at import\none = None\nsubset_csv = None    # DATALIMIT_SUBSET.csv path, or None on the full dataset; set by connect()\nbrain_regions = BrainRegions()\n\n\ndef connect(datadir):\n    \"\"\"Point the module-level ONE client at the cache under datadir.\n\n    An Alyx-backed client is required: eid2pid, which load_neural() uses to find the probe\n    insertions, exists only on OneAlyx. mode='local' returns a plain One without it. The\n    client also resolves each dataset's current revision, which is what is on disk -- the\n    release tables list the pre-revision paths, so resolving from them alone finds no\n    trials. The metadata that resolution needs is cached under one_cache/.rest, so a run\n    does not have to reach Alyx.\n\n    datadir: directory holding the one_cache/ folder.\n    Sets the globals `one` and `subset_csv`; returns nothing.\n    \"\"\"\n    global one\n    global subset_csv\n    cache = os.path.join(datadir, ONE_CACHE_DIRNAME)\n    tables = os.path.join(cache, RELEASE)\n    client = dict(base_url=BASE_URL, silent=True, cache_dir=cache, tables_dir=tables,\n                  mode='remote')\n    try:\n        # No password: passing one forces re-authentication over the network, while the\n        # token the fetch cached needs no connection.\n        one = ONE(**client)\n    except Exception:\n        one = ONE(password=PASSWORD, **client)\n    one.alyx.default_expiry = REST_EXPIRY\n    # One.load_cache reads the parquet tables where they are; the OneAlyx override would\n    # ask Alyx for a newer copy first. In 'remote' mode nothing is loaded until this is\n    # called, and search() then returns no sessions at all, without raising.\n    One.load_cache(one, tables_dir=tables)\n    # Only the datalimit dataset ships this file; on the full dataset there is nothing to\n    # restrict to, and subset_csv stays None.\n    path = os.path.join(datadir, 'DATALIMIT_SUBSET.csv')\n    subset_csv = path if os.path.isfile(path) else None\n\n\n# --- sessions ---\n\ndef select_sessions():\n    \"\"\"Released sessions that have everything the decoder needs, grouped by subject.\"\"\"\n\n    required = ['spikes.times.npy', 'spikes.clusters.npy',          # ephys\n                '_ibl_trials.table.pqt',                            # task\n                '_ibl_wheel.position.npy', '_ibl_wheel.timestamps.npy']\n\n    keep = set(one.search(query_type='local', datasets=required))\n    keep &= (set(one.search(query_type='local', datasets=['leftCamera.ROIMotionEnergy.npy',\n                                                          '_ibl_leftCamera.times.npy']))\n             | set(one.search(query_type='local', datasets=['rightCamera.ROIMotionEnergy.npy',\n                                                            '_ibl_rightCamera.times.npy'])))\n    # The datalimit dataset ships a subset of the release (see DATALIMIT_SUBSET.csv). The ONE\n    # release index is unmodified and still lists every released session, so\n    # restrict to the eids named there -- otherwise a session that is not on disk\n    # would be fetched over the network. The full dataset has no such file, and every\n    # session the index lists is on disk.\n    # Compare in string space: one.search returns UUID objects while the csv column\n    # is str, so a plain set intersection is empty whatever the contents. keep must\n    # stay UUID-valued for the `eid in keep` test below.\n    if subset_csv is not None:\n        subset_eids = set(pd.read_csv(subset_csv).eid.astype(str))\n        keep = {eid for eid in keep if str(eid) in subset_eids}\n        if not keep:\n            raise SystemExit(f'No sessions matched {subset_csv}; refusing to continue.')\n\n    eids, info = one.search(query_type='local', details=True)\n    selected = {}\n    for eid, session in zip(eids, info):\n        if eid in keep:\n            selected.setdefault(session['subject'], []).append(str(eid))\n    return {subject: selected[subject] for subject in sorted(selected)}\n\n\ndef camera_view(eid):\n    \"\"\"The side camera this session has whisker motion energy for, left preferred.\"\"\"\n    for view in ('left', 'right'):\n        if len(one.list_datasets(eid, filename=f'{view}Camera.ROIMotionEnergy.npy',\n                                 query_type='local')):\n            return view\n    raise ValueError(f'no whisker motion energy for {eid}')\n\n\n# --- trials ---\n\ndef trial_variables(trials):\n    \"\"\"Choice, block prior and trial number in block, one row per trial.\"\"\"\n    # probabilityLeft is constant within a block, so a change of value starts a new block\n    block = (trials.probabilityLeft != trials.probabilityLeft.shift()).cumsum()\n\n    return pd.DataFrame({'choice': trials.choice.map(CHOICE),\n                         'prior': trials.probabilityLeft.map(PRIOR),\n                         'trial_in_block': trials.groupby(block).cumcount()})\n\n\ndef covered(times, stim_on):\n    \"\"\"Trials whose window is spanned by this stream, allowing the one bin gap the reference allows.\"\"\"\n    start, stop = stim_on + T_START, stim_on + T_STOP\n    begin = np.searchsorted(times, start, side='right')      # first sample inside the window\n    end = np.searchsorted(times, stop, side='left') - 1       # last sample inside the window\n\n    # a trial with no sample at all, or a gap at either edge, has no usable trace\n    inside = begin <= end\n    first = np.where(inside, times[np.clip(begin, 0, times.size - 1)], np.inf)\n    last = np.where(inside, times[np.clip(end, 0, times.size - 1)], -np.inf)\n    return inside & (np.abs(start - first) <= BIN) & (np.abs(stop - last) <= BIN)\n\n\ndef usable_trials(trials, wheel_times, camera_times):\n    \"\"\"Trials the paper would keep, that also have a full wheel and camera window.\"\"\"\n    stim_on = trials.stimOn_times.to_numpy()\n    reaction = trials.firstMovement_times.to_numpy() - stim_on\n\n    keep = (reaction >= MIN_RT) & (reaction <= MAX_RT)          # NaN compares false, so those go too\n    keep &= trials.choice.isin(CHOICE).to_numpy()               # drops the no response trials\n    keep &= trials.probabilityLeft.isin(PRIOR).to_numpy()\n\n    return keep & covered(wheel_times, stim_on) & covered(camera_times, stim_on)\n\n\n# --- behavior ---\n\ndef window_slice(times, stim_on):\n    \"\"\"Index range of each trial window, so a stream is sliced rather than rescanned per trial.\"\"\"\n    window_start = stim_on + T_START\n    window_stop = stim_on + T_STOP\n\n    # the samples are in time order, so each window is one contiguous range of them\n    begin = np.searchsorted(times, window_start)\n    end = np.searchsorted(times, window_stop)\n\n    return begin, end\n\n\ndef trial_traces(times, values, stim_on):\n    \"\"\"A continuous trace resampled onto the same time grid for every trial.\"\"\"\n    begin, end = window_slice(times, stim_on)\n\n    traces = []\n    for trial, onset in enumerate(stim_on):\n        # one sample either side of the window, so the first and last bin centre are\n        # interpolated rather than clamped to the nearest sample inside it\n        first = max(begin[trial] - 1, 0)\n        last = end[trial] + 1\n\n        traces.append(np.interp(onset + TIME, times[first:last], values[first:last]))\n\n    return np.array(traces)\n\n\ndef discretize(trace):\n    \"\"\"Equal sized classes, split at percentiles of this session's own trace.\"\"\"\n    return np.digitize(trace, np.percentile(trace, SPLIT))\n\n\n# --- neural ---\n\ndef load_neural(eid):\n    \"\"\"Spike times and good clusters of a session, merged over its probes.\"\"\"\n    times, units, regions = [], [], []\n    for pid, name in zip(*one.eid2pid(eid)):\n        loader = SpikeSortingLoader(pid=pid, one=one, eid=eid, pname=name)\n\n        # skip the md5 check, which rereads every spike file and draws its own progress bar\n        spikes, clusters, channels = loader.load_spike_sorting(check_hash=False)\n\n        # a session can hold an insertion whose spike sorting was never released\n        if not clusters:\n            continue\n\n        clusters = loader.merge_clusters(spikes, clusters, channels)   # attaches metrics and histology\n\n        # `void` is the atlas label for a channel the histology placed outside the brain,\n        # so a unit there is not in a brain region at all\n        in_brain = brain_regions.acronym2acronym(\n            clusters['acronym'], mapping='Beryl') != 'void'\n\n        # spikes.clusters holds the row of the cluster table, so a per cluster flag\n        # can be indexed by it directly to say whether each spike survives\n        is_good = (clusters['label'] >= QC_LABEL) & in_brain\n        keep = is_good[spikes['clusters']]\n\n        # renumber the surviving clusters, continuing the count from the previous probe\n        new_index = np.cumsum(is_good) - 1\n        units.append(new_index[spikes['clusters'][keep]] + len(regions))\n        times.append(spikes['times'][keep])\n\n        # Beryl is the coarser atlas the reference code uses, e.g. DG-sg and DG-mo both become DG\n        regions.extend(brain_regions.acronym2acronym(clusters['acronym'][is_good], mapping='Beryl'))\n\n    times, units = np.concatenate(times), np.concatenate(units)\n\n    # sort the merged probes by spike time, as the reference does, so trials can be sliced\n    order = np.argsort(times, kind='stable')\n    return times[order], units[order], np.array(regions)\n\n\ndef spike_counts(times, units, n_units, stim_on):\n    \"\"\"Spike count per unit and bin, for every trial.\"\"\"\n    counts = np.empty((stim_on.size, n_units, N_BINS))\n    begin, end = window_slice(times, stim_on)\n\n    for trial, onset in enumerate(stim_on):\n        spike_time = times[begin[trial]:end[trial]] - onset      # time from stimulus onset\n        spike_unit = units[begin[trial]:end[trial]]\n\n        bin_index = np.floor((spike_time - T_START) / BIN).astype(np.int64)\n        \n        # a spike sitting on the window edge can round out of range once the onset is subtracted\n        bin_index = np.clip(bin_index, 0, N_BINS - 1)\n\n        # one flat index per spike lets a single bincount fill the whole unit by bin grid\n        flat_index = spike_unit * N_BINS + bin_index\n        counts[trial] = np.bincount(flat_index, minlength=n_units * N_BINS).reshape(n_units, N_BINS)\n\n    return counts\n\n\n# --- session ---\n\ndef process_session(eid, subject):\n    \"\"\"One session as lists of per trial neural, input and output arrays.\"\"\"\n    loader = SessionLoader(one=one, eid=eid)\n    loader.load_trials()\n    loader.load_wheel()\n    view = camera_view(eid)\n    loader.load_motion_energy(views=[view])\n\n    trials, wheel = loader.trials, loader.wheel\n    motion_energy = loader.motion_energy[f'{view}Camera']\n\n    variables = trial_variables(trials)                 # before filtering, so the block count is real\n    keep = usable_trials(trials, wheel.times.to_numpy(), motion_energy.times.to_numpy())\n    variables = variables[keep]\n    stim_on = trials.stimOn_times.to_numpy()[keep]\n\n    # no trial survived the mask, so there is nothing to load or bin\n    if stim_on.size == 0:\n        return {'session_id': str(eid), 'subject': subject, 'camera': view,\n                'neural': [], 'input': [], 'output': [],\n                'n_trials': 0, 'n_units': 0, 'regions': np.array([])}\n\n    speed = trial_traces(wheel.times, np.abs(wheel.velocity), stim_on)\n    whisker = trial_traces(motion_energy.times, motion_energy.iloc[:, 1], stim_on)\n\n    spike_times, spike_units, regions = load_neural(eid)\n    rate = spike_counts(spike_times, spike_units, regions.size, stim_on) / BIN\n\n    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   218→# binning / interpolation\n   219→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   220→def bin_spikes(spike_times, spike_clusters, n_neurons, interval_begs, binsize=0.02,\n   221→               n_bins=N_BINS):\n   222→    \"\"\"Spike counts in non-overlapping bins for every interval.\n   223→\n   224→    Reproduces ``bincount2D(times, clusters, xbin=binsize, xlim=[t_beg, t_end])``\n   225→    truncated to the first ``n_bins`` columns: bin ``k`` of a trial covers\n   226→    ``[t_beg + k*binsize, t_beg + (k+1)*binsize)`` and only spikes with\n   227→    ``t_beg <= t < t_end`` are counted.\n   228→\n   229→    `spike_times` must be sorted and `spike_clusters` must already be remapped\n   230→    to 0..n_neurons-1.\n   231→\n   232→    Returns (n_intervals, n_neurons, n_bins) float32 array of counts.\n   233→    \"\"\"\n   234→    n_intervals = len(interval_begs)\n   235→    out = np.zeros((n_intervals, n_neurons, n_bins), dtype=np.float32)\n   236→    interval_ends = interval_begs + n_bins * binsize\n   237→    i0s = np.searchsorted(spike_times, interval_begs, side='left')\n   238→    i1s = np.searchsorted(spike_times, interval_ends, side='left')\n   239→    for k in range(n_intervals):\n   240→        i0, i1 = i0s[k], i1s[k]\n   241→        if i1 <= i0:\n   242→            continue\n   243→        rel = spike_times[i0:i1] - interval_begs[k]\n   244→        bin_idx = np.floor(rel / binsize).astype(np.int64)\n   245→        np.clip(bin_idx, 0, n_bins - 1, out=bin_idx)\n   246→        flat = spike_clusters[i0:i1].astype(np.int64) * n_bins + bin_idx\n   247→        counts = np.bincount(flat, minlength=n_neurons * n_bins)\n   248→        out[k] = counts.reshape(n_neurons, n_bins)\n   249→    return out\n   250→\n   251→\n   252→def interpolate_behavior(times, values, interval_begs, binsize=0.02, n_bins=N_BINS):\n   253→    \"\"\"Interpolate a continuous behaviour onto each trial's bin grid.\n   254→\n   255→    Follows ``ibl_data_utils.get_behavior_per_interval``: samples strictly\n   256→    inside the interval are selected, then linearly interpolated (with\n   257→    extrapolation) onto ``linspace(t_beg + binsize, t_end, n_bins)``, i.e. the\n   258→    right edge of each spike bin.\n   259→\n   260→    Returns\n   261→    -------\n   262→    vals : (n_intervals, n_bins) float array (rows of NaN where invalid)\n   263→    good : (n_intervals,) bool array\n   264→    reasons : list of str or None, per interval\n   265→    \"\"\"\n   266→    interval_ends = interval_begs + n_bins * binsize\n   267→    idxs_beg = np.searchsorted(times, interval_begs, side='right')\n   268→    idxs_end = np.searchsorted(times, interval_ends, side='left')\n   269→\n   270→    n = len(interval_begs)\n   271→    vals = np.full((n, n_bins), np.nan, dtype=np.float64)\n   272→    good = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)\n   273→    reasons = [None] * n\n   274→\n   275→    for k in range(n):\n   276→        t = times[idxs_beg[k]:idxs_end[k]]\n   277→        v = values[idxs_beg[k]:idxs_end[k]]\n   278→        if len(v) == 0:\n   279→            reasons[k] = 'target data not present'\n   280→            continue\n   281→        if np.isnan(interval_begs[k]) or np.isnan(interval_ends[k]):\n   282→            reasons[k] = 'bad interval data'\n   283→            continue\n   284→        if np.abs(interval_begs[k] - t[0]) > binsize:\n   285→            reasons[k] = 'target data starts too late'\n   286→            continue\n   287→        if np.abs(interval_ends[k] - t[-1]) > binsize:\n   288→            reasons[k] = 'target data ends too early'\n   289→            continue\n   290→        if np.any(np.isnan(v)):\n   291→            # the reference keeps these (allow_nans=True) and imputes the trial\n   292→            # mean inside its data loader; a categorical target cannot be\n   293→            # imputed without inventing a class, so the trial is dropped\n   294→            reasons[k] = 'nans in target data'\n   295→            continue\n   296→        x = np.linspace(interval_begs[k] + binsize, interval_ends[k], n_bins)\n   297→        vals[k] = interp1d(t, v, kind='linear', fill_value='extrapolate')(x)\n   298→        good[k] = True\n   299→    return vals, good, reasons\n   300→\n   301→\n   302→def discretize_tertiles(values):\n   303→    \"\"\"Map a (n_trials, n_bins) continuous array onto {0, 1, 2} by tertile.\n   304→\n   305→    Thresholds are the 33.3 / 66.7 percentiles of all values pooled over the\n   306→    session's retained trials, so each session's classes are balanced and the\n   307→    arbitrary units of motion energy do not have to be comparable across\n   308→    sessions.\n   309→    \"\"\"\n   310→    flat = values.reshape(-1)\n   311→    q1, q2 = np.quantile(flat, TERTILES)\n   312→    if not (q1 < q2):\n   313→        # degenerate (heavily tied) distribution: fall back to the distinct\n   314→        # quantile values that do exist so the labels stay monotone\n   315→        edges = np.unique([q1, q2])\n   316→        labels = np.digitize(values, edges, right=False)\n   317→        return labels.astype(np.int64), (float(q1), float(q2))\n   318→    labels = np.digitize(values, [q1, q2], right=False).astype(np.int64)\n   319→    return labels, (float(q1), float(q2))\n   320→\n   321→\n   322→def trial_number_in_block(probability_left):\n   323→    \"\"\"0-based index of each trial within its constant-``probabilityLeft`` block.\n   324→\n   325→    Computed on the *unfiltered* trials table, because the block structure is a\n   326→    property of the experiment, not of which trials survive curation.\n   327→    \"\"\"\n   328→    p = np.asarray(probability_left, dtype=float)\n   329→    # a change (including to/from NaN) starts a new block\n   330→    changed = np.ones(len(p), dtype=bool)\n   331→    if len(p) > 1:\n   332→        same = (p[1:] == p[:-1]) | (np.isnan(p[1:]) & np.isnan(p[:-1]))\n   333→        changed[1:] = ~same\n   334→    block_id = np.cumsum(changed) - 1\n   335→    # index within block = position - position of first trial of that block\n   336→    first_of_block = np.zeros(block_id[-1] + 1, dtype=np.int64)\n   337→    starts = np.nonzero(changed)[0]\n   338→    first_of_block[:] = starts\n   339→    return np.arange(len(p)) - first_of_block[block_id]\n   340→\n   341→\n   342→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   343→# per-session conversion\n   344→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   345→def process_session(task):\n   346→    \"\"\"Convert one session. Returns a dict (or a dict with 'error').\"\"\"\n   347→    eid, probe_rows, subject, lab, show_processing = task\n   348→    t_start = time.time()\n   349→    timings = {}\n   350→    try:\n   351→        warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n   352→        one = get_one()\n   353→\n   354→        # ---- neural -------------------------------------------------------\n   355→        t0 = time.time()\n   356→        spikes, clusters = load_session_spikes(one, eid, probe_rows)\n   357→        timings['load_spikes'] = time.time() - t0\n   358→\n   359→        keep_idx, beryl, n_good = select_neurons(clusters)\n   360→        n_neurons = len(keep_idx)\n   361→        if n_neurons < MIN_NEURONS_PER_SESSION:\n   362→            return {'eid': eid,\n   363→                    'error': f'only {n_neurons} well-isolated grey-matter neurons '\n   364→                             f'(< {MIN_NEURONS_PER_SESSION})'}\n   365→\n   366→        # remap cluster ids of the retained spikes to 0..n_neurons-1\n   367→        remap = np.full(len(clusters), -1, dtype=np.int64)\n   368→        remap[keep_idx] = np.arange(n_neurons)\n   369→        spike_cl = remap[spikes['clusters']]\n   370→        sel = spike_cl >= 0\n   371→        spike_times = np.ascontiguousarray(spikes['times'][sel])\n   372→        spike_cl = np.ascontiguousarray(spike_cl[sel])\n   373→\n   374→        # ---- trials -------------------------------------------------------\n   375→        t0 = time.time()\n   376→        sess_loader, trials, trials_mask = load_trials(one, eid)\n   377→        timings['load_trials'] = time.time() - t0\n   378→        n_trials_raw = len(trials)\n   379→\n   380→        stim_on_all = trials[PARAMS['align_time']].to_numpy()\n   381→        tib_all = trial_number_in_block(trials['probabilityLeft'].to_numpy())\n   382→\n   383→        cand = np.nonzero(trials_mask)[0]\n   384→        if len(cand) == 0:\n   385→            return {'eid': eid, 'error': 'no trials pass the reference trial mask'}\n   386→        interval_begs = stim_on_all[cand] + PARAMS['time_window'][0]\n   387→\n   388→        # ---- continuous behaviours ---------------------------------------\n   389→        t0 = time.time()\n   390→        beh = load_continuous_behaviors(one, eid, sess_loader)\n   391→        timings['load_behavior'] = time.time() - t0\n   392→\n   393→        t0 = time.time()\n   394→        beh_vals, beh_good, beh_reasons = {}, {}, {}\n   395→        for name, d in beh.items():\n   396→            v, g, r = interpolate_behavior(d['times'], d['values'], interval_begs,\n   397→                                           PARAMS['binsize'], N_BINS)\n   398→            beh_vals[name], beh_good[name], beh_reasons[name] = v, g, r\n   399→        timings['bin_behavior'] = time.time() - t0\n   400→\n   401→        keep_trial = np.ones(len(cand), dtype=bool)\n   402→        for name in beh_vals:\n   403→            keep_trial &= beh_good[name]\n   404→        if keep_trial.sum() < 2:\n   405→            return {'eid': eid,\n   406→                    'error': f'only {int(keep_trial.sum())} trials with valid behaviour'}\n   407→\n   408→        trial_idx = cand[keep_trial]\n   409→        interval_begs = interval_begs[keep_trial]\n   410→        n_trials = len(trial_idx)\n   411→\n   412→        # ---- bin spikes ---------------------------------------------------\n   413→        t0 = time.time()\n   414→        binned = bin_spikes(spike_times, spike_cl, n_neurons, interval_begs,\n   415→                            PARAMS['binsize'], N_BINS)\n   416→        timings['bin_spikes'] = time.time() - t0\n   417→\n   418→        # Drop trials in which not a single spike was recorded from the whole\n   419→        # population over the 2 s window. With >= 5 simultaneously recorded\n   420→        # neurons this indicates a dropout in the recording rather than a\n   421→        # genuinely silent population, and such a trial carries no information\n   422→        # for a neural decoder.\n   423→        has_spikes = binned.any(axis=(1, 2))\n   424→        n_zero_spike = int((~has_spikes).sum())\n   425→        if n_zero_spike:\n   426→            binned = binned[has_spikes]\n   427→            trial_idx = trial_idx[has_spikes]\n   428→            interval_begs = interval_begs[has_spikes]\n   429→            keep_trial[np.nonzero(keep_trial)[0][~has_spikes]] = False\n   430→            n_trials = len(trial_idx)\n   431→            if n_trials < 2:\n   432→                return {'eid': eid, 'error': 'fewer than 2 trials with recorded spikes'}\n   433→\n   434→        # ---- inputs -------------------------------------------------------\n   435→        # right edge of each spike bin, relative to stimulus onset\n   436→        bin_times = PARAMS['time_window'][0] + PARAMS['binsize'] * np.arange(1, N_BINS + 1)\n   437→        tib = tib_all[trial_idx].astype(np.float32)\n   438→        inputs = np.empty((n_trials, 2, N_BINS), dtype=np.float32)\n   439→        inputs[:, 0, :] = bin_times[None, :]\n   440→        inputs[:, 1, :] = tib[:, None]\n   441→\n   442→        # ---- outputs ------------------------------------------------------\n   443→        choice = trials['choice'].to_numpy()[trial_idx]\n   444→        # choice == +1 is a LEFT report, choice == -1 a RIGHT report\n   445→        choice_lbl = ((1 - choice) / 2).astype(np.int64)\n   446→\n   447→        pleft = trials['probabilityLeft'].to_numpy()[trial_idx]\n   448→        prior_lbl = np.full(n_trials, -1, dtype=np.int64)\n   449→        for val, lbl in ((0.2, 0), (0.5, 1), (0.8, 2)):\n   450→            prior_lbl[np.isclose(pleft, val)] = lbl\n   451→        if np.any(prior_lbl < 0):\n   452→            bad = np.unique(pleft[prior_lbl < 0])\n   453→            return {'eid': eid, 'error': f'unexpected probabilityLeft values {bad}'}\n   454→\n   455→        ws = beh_vals['wheel-speed'][keep_trial]\n   456→        me = beh_vals['whisker-motion-energy'][keep_trial]\n   457→        ws_lbl, ws_thr = discretize_tertiles(ws)\n   458→        me_lbl, me_thr = discretize_tertiles(me)\n   459→\n   460→        outputs = np.empty((n_trials, 4, N_BINS), dtype=np.int64)\n   461→        outputs[:, 0, :] = choice_lbl[:, None]\n   462→        outputs[:, 1, :] = prior_lbl[:, None]\n   463→        outputs[:, 2, :] = ws_lbl\n   464→        outputs[:, 3, :] = me_lbl\n   465→\n   466→        if show_processing:\n   467→            try:\n   468→                make_processing_figure(\n   469→                    eid, trials, trial_idx, interval_begs, binned, inputs, outputs,\n   470→                    beh, ws, me, ws_thr, me_thr, bin_times, beryl, tib_all, trials_mask)\n   471→            except Exception:\n   472→                traceback.print_exc()\n   473→\n   474→        result = {\n   475→            'eid': eid,\n   476→            'subject': subject,\n   477→            'lab': lab,\n   478→            'neural': [binned[k] for k in range(n_trials)],\n   479→            'input': [inputs[k] for k in range(n_trials)],\n   480→            'output': [outputs[k] for k in range(n_trials)],\n   481→            'beryl': beryl,\n   482→            'info': {\n   483→                'eid': eid,\n   484→                'subject': subject,\n   485→                'lab': lab,\n   486→                'n_probes': int(len(probe_rows)),\n   487→                'n_clusters_total': int(len(clusters)),\n   488→                'n_good_units': n_good,\n   489→                'n_neurons': int(n_neurons),\n   490→                'n_trials_raw': int(n_trials_raw),\n   491→                'n_trials_mask': int(trials_mask.sum()),\n   492→                'n_trials': int(n_trials),\n   493→                'n_zero_spike_trials_dropped': n_zero_spike,\n   494→                # row indices into the session's (unfiltered) trials table for\n   495→                # every retained trial, so the conversion can be re-derived and\n   496→                # checked against the raw ONE objects\n   497→                'trial_idx': trial_idx.astype(np.int32),\n   498→                'whisker_source': beh['whisker-motion-energy']['source'],\n   499→                'wheel_speed_tertiles': ws_thr,\n   500→                'whisker_me_tertiles': me_thr,\n   501→                'mean_firing_rate_hz': float(binned.sum() / (n_neurons * n_trials * 2.0)),\n   502→                'behavior_drop_counts': {\n   503→                    name: int((~beh_good[name]).sum()) for name in beh_good},\n   504→            },\n   505→            'timings': timings,\n   506→            'total_time': time.time() - t_start,\n   507→        }\n   508→        return result\n   509→    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 - one bad session must not kill the run\n   510→        return {'eid': eid, 'error': f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}',\n   511→                'traceback': traceback.format_exc()}\n   512→\n   513→\n   514→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   515→# diagnostic plots\n   516→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   517→def make_processing_figure(eid, trials, trial_idx, interval_begs, binned, inputs,\n   518→                           outputs, beh, ws, me, ws_thr, me_thr, bin_times, beryl,\n   519→                           tib_all, trials_mask):\n   520→    \"\"\"Save a figure showing every processing step for one session.\"\"\"\n   521→    import matplotlib\n   522→    matplotlib.use('Agg')\n   523→    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n   524→\n   525→    n_trials = len(trial_idx)\n   526→    ex = min(3, n_trials - 1)  # example trial (index into the retained trials)\n   527→    t_beg = interval_begs[ex]\n   528→    stim_on = trials['stimOn_times'].to_numpy()[trial_idx[ex]]\n   529→\n   530→    fig, axes = plt.subplots(4, 2, figsize=(22, 18))\n   531→\n   532→    # (0,0) raw spike raster of the example trial with bin edges\n   533→    ax = axes[0, 0]\n   534→    ax.imshow(binned[ex], aspect='auto', interpolation='nearest', origin='lower',\n   535→              extent=[bin_times[0] - 0.02, bin_times[-1], 0, binned.shape[1]],\n   536→              cmap='Greys')\n   537→    ax.axvline(0, color='r', lw=2)\n   538→    ax.set_title(f'{eid[:8]} trial {ex}: binned spike counts (n={binned.shape[1]} neurons)')\n   539→    ax.set_xlabel('time from stimulus onset (s)')\n   540→    ax.set_ylabel('neuron')\n   541→\n   542→    # (0,1) population PSTH over all trials -> should show a stimulus response at t=0\n   543→    ax = axes[0, 1]\n   544→    psth = binned.mean(axis=(0, 1)) / 0.02\n   545→    ax.plot(bin_times, psth, 'k')\n   546→    ax.axvline(0, color='r', lw=2, label='stimulus onset')\n   547→    ax.set_title('population PSTH (mean firing rate over all trials/neurons)')\n   548→    ax.set_xlabel('time from stimulus onset (s)')\n   549→    ax.set_ylabel('rate (Hz)')\n   550→    ax.legend()\n   551→\n   552→    # (1,0) wheel speed: raw trace vs interpolated bins for the example trial\n   553→    ax = axes[1, 0]\n   554→    d = beh['wheel-speed']\n   555→    m = (d['times'] >= t_beg - 0.1) & (d['times'] <= t_beg + 2.1)\n   556→    ax.plot(d['times'][m] - stim_on, d['values'][m], color='0.6', label='raw |velocity|')\n   557→    ax.plot(bin_times, ws[ex], 'b.-', label='interpolated to bin edges')\n   558→    for thr in ws_thr:\n   559→        ax.axhline(thr, color='g', ls='--', lw=1)\n   560→    ax.axvline(0, color='r', lw=2)\n   561→    ax.set_title('wheel speed: raw vs binned (green = session tertiles)')\n   562→    ax.set_xlabel('time from stimulus onset (s)')\n   563→    ax.legend()\n   564→\n   565→    # (1,1) wheel speed discretisation\n   566→    ax = axes[1, 1]\n   567→    ax.plot(bin_times, ws[ex], 'b.-', label='wheel speed')\n   568→    ax2 = ax.twinx()\n   569→    ax2.step(bin_times, outputs[ex, 2], where='mid', color='m', label='class')\n   570→    ax2.set_ylim(-0.2, 2.2)\n   571→    ax2.set_ylabel('class (0/1/2)', color='m')\n   572→    for thr in ws_thr:\n   573→        ax.axhline(thr, color='g', ls='--', lw=1)\n   574→    ax.axvline(0, color='r', lw=2)\n   575→    ax.set_title('wheel speed discretisation check')\n   576→    ax.set_xlabel('time from stimulus onset (s)')\n   577→\n   578→    # (2,0) whisker motion energy raw vs interpolated\n   579→    ax = axes[2, 0]\n   580→    d = beh['whisker-motion-energy']\n   581→    m = (d['times'] >= t_beg - 0.1) & (d['times'] <= t_beg + 2.1)\n   582→    ax.plot(d['times'][m] - stim_on, d['values'][m], color='0.6', marker='.',\n   583→            label=f\"raw ({d['source']})\")\n   584→    ax.plot(bin_times, me[ex], 'b.-', label='interpolated to bin edges')\n   585→    for thr in me_thr:\n   586→        ax.axhline(thr, color='g', ls='--', lw=1)\n   587→    ax.axvline(0, color='r', lw=2)\n   588→    ax.set_title('whisker motion energy: raw vs binned')\n   589→    ax.set_xlabel('time from stimulus onset (s)')\n   590→    ax.legend()\n   591→\n   592→    # (2,1) whisker ME discretisation\n   593→    ax = axes[2, 1]\n   594→    ax.plot(bin_times, me[ex], 'b.-')\n   595→    ax2 = ax.twinx()\n   596→    ax2.step(bin_times, outputs[ex, 3], where='mid', color='m')\n   597→    ax2.set_ylim(-0.2, 2.2)\n   598→    ax2.set_ylabel('class (0/1/2)', color='m')\n   599→    for thr in me_thr:\n   600→        ax.axhline(thr, color='g', ls='--', lw=1)\n   601→    ax.axvline(0, color='r', lw=2)\n   602→    ax.set_title('whisker motion energy discretisation check')\n   603→    ax.set_xlabel('time from stimulus onset (s)')\n   604→\n   605→    # (3,0) block structure / trial-in-block input\n   606→    ax = axes[3, 0]\n   607→    ax.plot(trials['probabilityLeft'].to_numpy(), 'k-', label='probabilityLeft')\n   608→    ax.plot(tib_all / 100.0, 'c-', lw=1, label='trial-in-block / 100')\n   609→    keep_all = np.zeros(len(trials), dtype=bool)\n   610→    keep_all[trial_idx] = True\n   611→    ax.plot(np.nonzero(keep_all)[0], np.full(keep_all.sum(), 1.05), '|', color='g',\n   612→            ms=4, label='retained trials')\n   613→    ax.plot(np.nonzero(trials_mask & ~keep_all)[0],\n   614→            np.full(int((trials_mask & ~keep_all).sum()), 1.12), '|', color='orange',\n   615→            ms=4, label='dropped (behaviour)')\n   616→    ax.set_title('block structure and trial curation')\n   617→    ax.set_xlabel('trial index (unfiltered)')\n   618→    ax.legend(fontsize=8)\n   619→\n   620→    # (3,1) outputs / inputs summary over trials\n   621→    ax = axes[3, 1]\n   622→    ax.imshow(outputs[:, 2, :], aspect='auto', interpolation='nearest', cmap='viridis',\n   623→              extent=[bin_times[0] - 0.02, bin_times[-1], n_trials, 0])\n   624→    ax.axvline(0, color='r', lw=2)\n   625→    ax.set_title('wheel-speed class over all trials (rows = trials)')\n   626→    ax.set_xlabel('time from stimulus onset (s)')\n   627→    ax.set_ylabel('trial')\n   628→\n   629→    fig.suptitle(f'Processing steps for session {eid}   '\n   630→                 f'({n_trials} trials, {binned.shape[1]} neurons, '\n   631→                 f'{len(np.unique(beryl))} Beryl regions)')\n   632→    fig.tight_layout()\n   633→    fig.savefig(f'processing_{eid}.png', dpi=110)\n   634→    plt.close(fig)\n   635→    print(f'  wrote processing_{eid}.png')\n   636→\n   637→\n   638→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   639→# driver\n   640→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   641→def main():\n   642→    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__,\n   643→                                 formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)\n   644→    ap.add_argument('outfile', type=str)\n   645→    mode = ap.add_mutually_exclusive_group()\n   646→    mode.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', default=True)\n   647→    mode.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true')\n   648→    ap.add_argument('--show-processing', action='store_true')\n   649→    ap.add_argument('--n-workers', type=int, default=24)\n   650→    args = ap.parse_args()\n   651→\n   652→    t_start = time.time()\n   653→    bwm = pd.read_csv(BWM_FREEZE, index_col=0)\n   654→    eids = list(dict.fromkeys(bwm.eid.tolist()))  # preserve freeze order\n   655→    if args.sample:\n   656→        eids = eids[:2]\n   657→    print(f'BWM freeze: {len(bwm)} insertions, {bwm.eid.nunique()} sessions, '\n   658→          f'{bwm.subject.nunique()} subjects')\n   659→    print(f'Processing {len(eids)} sessions with {args.n_workers} workers')\n   660→\n   661→    n_show = 2 if args.show_processing else 0\n   662→    tasks = []\n   663→    for i, eid in enumerate(eids):\n   664→        rows = bwm[bwm.eid == eid]\n   665→        tasks.append((eid, rows[['pid', 'probe_name']].copy(),\n   666→                      rows.subject.iloc[0], rows.lab.iloc[0], i < n_show))\n   667→\n   668→    results = []\n   669→    if args.n_workers <= 1:\n   670→        for t in tasks:\n   671→            results.append(process_session(t))\n   672→            report(results[-1], len(results), len(tasks))\n   673→    else:\n   674→        import multiprocessing as mp\n   675→        ctx = mp.get_context('fork')\n   676→        with ctx.Pool(processes=min(args.n_workers, len(tasks))) as pool:\n   677→            for res in pool.imap_unordered(process_session, tasks):\n   678→                results.append(res)\n   679→                report(res, len(results), len(tasks))\n   680→\n   681→    # ---- assemble -------------------------------------------------------\n   682→    ok = [r for r in results if 'error' not in r]\n   683→    failed = [r for r in results if 'error' in r]\n   684→    # deterministic session order (freeze order)\n   685→    order = {eid: i for i, eid in enumerate(eids)}\n   686→    ok.sort(key=lambda r: order[r['eid']])\n   687→\n   688→    print(f'\\n{len(ok)} sessions converted, {len(failed)} skipped')\n   689→    for r in failed:\n   690→        print(f\"  SKIPPED {r['eid']}: {r['error']}\")\n   691→\n   692→    subjects = sorted({r['subject'] for r in ok})\n   693→    subject_index = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(subjects)}\n   694→    regions = sorted({a for r in ok for a in np.unique(r['beryl'])})\n   695→    region_index = {a: i for i, a in enumerate(regions)}\n   696→\n   697→    data = {\n   698→        'neural': [r['neural'] for r in ok],\n   699→        'input': [r['input'] for r in ok],\n   700→        'output': [r['output'] for r in ok],\n   701→        'subjects': subjects,\n   702→        'subject_idx': np.array([subject_index[r['subject']] for r in ok], dtype=np.int64),\n   703→        'brain_regions': regions,\n   704→        'brain_region_idx': [np.array([region_index[a] for a in r['beryl']], dtype=np.int64)\n   705→                             for r in ok],\n   706→        'input_names': INPUT_NAMES,\n   707→        'output_names': OUTPUT_NAMES,\n   708→        'output_values': OUTPUT_VALUES,\n   709→        'metadata': {\n   710→            'dataset': 'IBL brain-wide map (BWM) public release',\n   711→            'source_papers': [\n   712→                'IBL et al., A brain-wide map of neural activity during complex behaviour',\n   713→                'Zhang et al. 2026 Neuron, Exploiting correlations across trials and '\n   714→                'behavioral sessions to improve neural decoding',\n   715→            ],\n   716→            'task_description': (\n   717→                'Head-fixed mice report the side of a visual grating by turning a wheel. '\n   718→                'Stimulus contrast is drawn from {0, 6.25, 12.5, 25, 100}%; after an initial '\n   719→                '90 unbiased trials the prior probability that the stimulus appears on the '\n   720→                'left alternates between 0.2 and 0.8 in uncued blocks of 20-100 trials. '\n   721→                'Decoded from 20 ms binned spike counts of well-isolated neurons: the '\n   722→                \"animal's choice (left/right), the block prior probability of left \"\n   723→                '(0.2/0.5/0.8), and wheel speed and whisker motion energy discretised into '\n   724→                'per-session tertiles at every 20 ms bin.'),\n   725→            'time_bin_size': PARAMS['binsize'] * 1000.0,  # ms\n   726→            'temporal_alignment_event': 'visual stimulus onset (trials.stimOn_times)',\n   727→            'off_start': PARAMS['time_window'][0],\n   728→            'off_end': PARAMS['time_window'][1],\n   729→            'n_timepoints': N_BINS,\n   730→            'neural_units': 'spike counts per 20 ms bin',\n   731→            'neuron_selection': (\"IBL well-isolated units (clusters.label >= 1: amplitude \"\n   732→                                 \"> 50 uV, noise cut-off < 20 uV, refractory-period \"\n   733→                                 \"violation) located in grey matter (Beryl acronym not in \"\n   734→                                 \"{root, void}); probes of a session merged\"),\n   735→            'trial_selection': ('brainbox load_trials_and_mask: no NaN in stimOn_times, '\n   736→                                'choice, feedback_times, probabilityLeft, '\n   737→                                'firstMovement_times, feedbackType; reaction time in '\n   738→                                '[0.08, 2.0] s; feedback_times - goCue_times <= 10 s; '\n   739→                                'choice != 0; plus full behavioural coverage of the '\n   740→                                '2 s window with no NaN'),\n   741→            'input_description': {\n   742→                'time_from_stim_onset_s': ('time (s) of the right edge of each 20 ms bin '\n   743→                                           'relative to stimulus onset, -0.48 ... 1.50'),\n   744→                'trial_number_in_block': ('0-based index of the trial within its constant-'\n   745→                                          'probabilityLeft block, counted on the '\n   746→                                          'unfiltered trials table'),\n   747→            },\n   748→            'output_description': {\n   749→                'choice': 'trials.choice: +1 (left report) -> 0, -1 (right report) -> 1',\n   750→                'prior': 'trials.probabilityLeft: 0.2 -> 0, 0.5 -> 1, 0.8 -> 2',\n   751→                'wheel_speed': ('|wheel velocity| interpolated to bin edges, discretised '\n   752→                                'at the session tertiles'),\n   753→                'whisker_motion_energy': ('left (else right) camera whisker-pad ROI motion '\n   754→                                          'energy interpolated to bin edges, discretised '\n   755→                                          'at the session tertiles'),\n   756→            },\n   757→            'session_info': [r['info'] for r in ok],\n   758→            'skipped_sessions': [{'eid': r['eid'], 'error': r['error']} for r in failed],\n   759→        },\n   760→    }\n   761→\n   762→    # ---- summary --------------------------------------------------------\n   763→    n_trials = [len(s) for s in data['neural']]\n   764→    n_neurons = [s[0].shape[0] for s in data['neural']]\n   765→ python3\n\"\"\"\nConvert the IBL brain-wide map (BWM) public dataset into the decoder-compatible\ndictionary format described in the task specification.\n\nUsage\n-----\n    python -u /app/convert_data.py <outpicklefile> [--full | --sample]\n                                   [--show-processing] [--n-workers N]\n\nOptions\n-------\n    --full             process every session of the BWM freeze (default)\n    --sample           process only 2 sessions (quick end-to-end test)\n    --show-processing  save a multi-panel diagnostic figure per session\n                       (at most 2 sessions) as processing_<eid>.png\n    --n-workers N      number of worker processes (default: 24)\n\nProcessing follows `code_zhang2025/src/0_data_caching.py` and\n`code_zhang2025/src/utils/ibl_data_utils.py`:\n\n  * trials aligned to ``stimOn_times`` over the window (-0.5, +1.5) s\n  * spikes binned into 20 ms non-overlapping bins  ->  T = 100\n  * trial velocity|, whisker motion\n    energy from the left camera, falling back to the right camera) linearly\n    interpolated onto the right edge of each spike bin, with the reference's\n    interval-coverage checks\n\nDeviations from the reference caching script, and why, are documented in\n/app/CONVERSION_NOTES.md (Step 4/5). The main ones:\n  * neurons are restricted to IBL well-isolated units (`label >= 1`) in grey\n    matter (Beryl acronym not in {root, void}), following the data paper\n  * trials whose behaviour window contains NaN are dropped instead of being\n    NaN-imputed at model-fit time\n  * the continuous behaviours are discretised into per-session tertiles, as\n    required by the categorical-output specification\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport os\nimport pickle\nimport sys\nimport time\nimport traceback\nimport warnings\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\n\n# reference code from the method paper\nsys.path.insert(0, '/app/code/code_zhang2025/src')\n\nfrom scipy.interpolate import interp1d\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# constants -- these mirror `params` in code_zhang2025/src/0_data_caching.py\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nPARAMS = {\n    'interval_len': 2.0,\n    'binsize': 0.02,\n    'single_region': False,\n    'align_time': 'stimOn_times',\n    'time_window': (-0.5, 1.5),\n}\nN_BINS = int(np.ceil(PARAMS['interval_len'] / PARAMS['binsize']))  # 100\n\nBWM_FREEZE = '/app/code/code_zhang2025/data/bwm_release.csv'\nONE_TABLES_DIR = '/app/data/one_cache/Brainwidemap'\nONE_BASE_URL = 'https://openalyx.internationalbrainlab.org'\n\n#: Beryl acronyms that are not grey-matter brain regions.\nNON_REGION_ACRONYMS = ('root', 'void')\n\n#: quantiles used to discretise the continuous behaviours into 3 classes\nTERTILES = (1.0 / 3.0, 2.0 / 3.0)\n\n#: minimum number of retained neurons for a session to be kept. (spike-time sorted, merged)\n    clusters : pandas.DataFrame, one row per cluster, re-indexed across probes\n    \"\"\"\n    from brainbox.io.one import SpikeSortingLoader\n    from pname=row.probe_name)\n        sp, cl, ch = ssl.load_spike_sorting()\n        cl_df = SpikeSortingLoader.merge_clusters(sp, cl, ch, compute_metrics=False).to_df()\n        cl_df['pid'] = row.pid\n        spikes_list.append(sp)\n        clusters_list.append(cl_df)\n    return merge_probes(spikes_list, clusters_list)\n\n\ndef select_neurons(clusters):\n    \"\"\"Well-isolated units (IBL ``label >= 1``) located in grey matter.\n\n    ``label`` is the fraction of the three RIGOR single-unit metrics passed\n    (amplitude > 50 uV, noise cut-off < 20 uV, refractory-period violation), so\n    ``label >= 1`` is exactly the data paper's \"well-isolated neuron\".\n\n    Returns\n    -------\n    keep_idx : (n_kept,) int array of row indices into `clusters`\n    beryl : (n_kept,) array of Beryl acronyms for the kept clusters\n    n_good : number of well-isolated units before the grey-matter restriction\n    \"\"\"\n    br = get_brain_regions()\n    beryl_all = np.asarray(br.acronym2acronym(clusters['acronym'].to_numpy(), mapping='Beryl'))\n    good = clusters['label'].to_numpy() >= 1\n    in_brain = ~np.isin(beryl_all, NON_REGION_ACRONYMS)\n    keep = good & in_brain\n    keep_idx = np.nonzero(keep)[0]\n    return keep_idx, beryl_all[keep_idx], int(good.sum())\n\n\ndef load_trials(one, eid):\n    \"\"\"Trials table + reference trial mask (``load_trials_and_mask``).\"\"\"\n    from brainbox.io.one import SessionLoader\n    from utils.ibl_data_utils import load_trials_and_mask\n\n    sess_loader = SessionLoader(one=one, eid=eid)\n    trials, mask = load_trials_and_mask(one=one, eid=eid, max_trial_len=10.0,\n                                        sess_loader=sess_loader)\n    return sess_loader, trials, mask.to_numpy()\n\n\ndef load_continuous_behaviors(one, eid, sess_loader):\n    \"\"\"Wheel speed and whisker motion energy for the whole session.\n\n    Same sources as ``ibl_data_utils.load_target_behavior``:\n    wheel speed = |velocity| from ``SessionLoader.load_wheel`` (1 kHz,\n    Gaussian-smoothed), whisker motion energy from the left camera ROI motion\n    energy, falling back to the right camera when the left is unavailable.\n    \"\"\"\n    beh = {}\n\n    sess_loader.load_wheel()\n    beh['wheel-speed'] = {\n        'times': sess_loader.wheel['times'].to_numpy(),\n        'values': np.abs(sess_loader.wheel['velocity'].to_numpy()),\n        'source': 'wheel.velocity (abs)',\n    }\n\n    me = None\n    for view, cam in (('left', 'leftCamera'), ('right', 'rightCamera')):\n        try:\n            sess_loader.load_motion_energy(views=[view])\n            df = sess_loader.motion_energy[cam]\n            me = {\n                'times': df['times'].to_numpy(),\n                'values': df['whiskerMotionEnergy'].to_numpy(),\n                'source': f'{cam}.ROIMotionEnergy',\n            }\n            break\n        except Exception:\n            continue\n    if me is None:\n        raise RuntimeError('no whisker motion energy available (left or right camera)')\n    beh['whisker-motion-energy'] = me\n    return beh\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# binning / interpolation\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef bin_spikes(spike_times, spike_clusters, n_neurons, interval_begs, binsize=0.02,\n               n_bins=N_BINS):\n    \"\"\"Spike counts in non-overlapping bins for every interval.\n\n    Reproduces ``bincount2D(times, clusters, xbin=binsize, xlim=[t_beg, t_end])``\n    truncated to the first ``n_bins`` columns: bin ``k`` of a trial covers\n    ``[t_beg + k*binsize, t_beg + (k+1)*binsize)`` and only spikes with\n    ``t_beg <= t < t_end`` are counted.\n\n    `spike_times` must be sorted and `spike_clusters` must already be remapped\n    to 0..n_neurons-1.\n\n    Returns (n_intervals, n_neurons, n_bins) float32 array of counts.\n    \"\"\"\n    n_intervals = len(interval_begs)\n    out = np.zeros((n_intervals, n_neurons, n_bins), dtype=np.float32)\n    interval_ends = interval_begs + n_bins * binsize\n    i0s = np.searchsorted(spike_times, interval_begs, side='left')\n    i1s = np.searchsorted(spike_times, interval_ends, side='left')\n    for k in range(n_intervals):\n        i0, i1 = i0s[k], i1s[k]\n        if i1 <= i0:\n            continue\n        rel = spike_times[i0:i1] - interval_begs[k]\n        bin_idx = np.floor(rel / binsize).astype(np.int64)\n        np.clip(bin_idx, 0, n_bins - 1, out=bin_idx)\n        flat = spike_clusters[i0:i1].astype(np.int64) * n_bins + bin_idx\n        counts = np.bincount(flat, minlength=n_neurons * n_bins)\n        out[k] = counts.reshape(n_neurons, n_bins)\n    return out\n\n\ndef interpolate_behavior(times, values, interval_begs, binsize=0.02, n_bins=N_BINS):\n    \"\"\"Interpolate a continuous behaviour onto each trial's bin grid.\n\n    Follows ``ibl_data_utils.get_behavior_per_interval``: samples strictly\n    inside the interval are selected, then linearly interpolated (with\n    extrapolation) onto ``linspace(t_beg + binsize, t_end, n_bins)``, i.e. the\n    right edge of each spike bin.\n\n    Returns\n    -------\n    vals : (n_intervals, n_bins) float array (rows of NaN where invalid)\n    good : (n_intervals,) bool array\n    reasons : list of str or None, per interval\n    \"\"\"\n    interval_ends = interval_begs + n_bins * binsize\n    idxs_beg = np.searchsorted(times, interval_begs, side='right')\n    idxs_end = np.searchsorted(times, interval_ends, side='left')\n\n    n = len(interval_begs)\n    vals = np.full((n, n_bins), np.nan, dtype=np.float64)\n    good = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)\n    reasons = [None] * n\n\n    for k in range(n):\n        t = times[idxs_beg[k]:idxs_end[k]]\n        v = values[idxs_beg[k]:idxs_end[k]]\n        if len(v) == 0:\n            reasons[k] = 'target data not present'\n            continue\n        if np.isnan(interval_begs[k]) or np.isnan(interval_ends[k]):\n            reasons[k] = 'bad interval data'\n            continue\n        if np.abs(interval_begs[k] - t[0]) > binsize:\n            reasons[k] = 'target data starts too late'\n            continue\n        if np.abs(interval_ends[k] - t[-1]) > binsize:\n            reasons[k] = 'target data ends too early'\n            continue\n        if np.any(np.isnan(v)):\n            # the reference keeps these (allow_nans=True) and imputes the trial\n            # mean inside its data loader; a categorical target cannot be\n            # imputed without inventing a class, so the trial is dropped\n            reasons[k] = 'nans in target data'\n            continue\n        x = np.linspace(interval_begs[k] + binsize, interval_ends[k], n_bins)\n        vals[k] = interp1d(t, v, kind='linear', fill_value='extrapolate')(x)\n        good[k] = True\n    return vals, good, reasons\n\n\ndef discretize_tertiles(values):\n    \"\"\"Map a (n_trials, n_bins) continuous array onto {0, 1, 2} by tertile.\n\n    Thresholds are the 33.3 / 66.7 percentiles of all values pooled over the\n    session's retained trials, so each session's classes are balanced and the\n    arbitrary units of motion energy do not have to be comparable across\n    sessions.\n    \"\"\"\n    flat = values.reshape(-1)\n    q1, q2 = np.quantile(flat, TERTILES)\n    if not (q1 < q2):\n        # degenerate (heavily tied) distribution: fall back to the distinct\n        # quantile values that do exist so the labels stay monotone\n        edges = np.unique([q1, q2])\n        labels = np.digitize(values, edges, right=False)\n        return labels.astype(np.int64), (float(q1), float(q2))\n    labels = np.digitize(values, [q1, q2], right=False).astype(np.int64)\n    return labels, (float(q1), float(q2))\n\n\ndef trial_number_in_block(probability_left):\n    \"\"\"0-based index of each trial within its constant-``probabilityLeft`` block.\n\n    Computed on the *unfiltered* trials table, because the block structure is a\n    property of the experiment, not of which trials survive curation.\n    \"\"\"\n    p = np.asarray(probability_left, dtype=float)\n    # a change (including to/from NaN) starts a new block\n    changed = np.ones(len(p), dtype=bool)\n    if len(p) > 1:\n        same = (p[1:] == p[:-1]) | (np.isnan(p[1:]) & np.isnan(p[:-1]))\n        changed[1:] = ~same\n    block_id = np.cumsum(changed) - 1\n    # index within block = position - position of first trial of that block\n    first_of_block = np.zeros(block_id[-1] + 1, dtype=np.int64)\n    starts = np.nonzero(changed)[0]\n    first_of_block[:] = starts\n    return np.arange(len(p)) - first_of_block[block_id]\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# per-session conversion\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef process_session(task):\n    \"\"\"Convert one session. Returns a dict (or a dict with 'error').\"\"\"\n    eid, probe_rows, subject, lab, show_processing = task\n    t_start = time.time()\n    timings = {}\n    try:\n        warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n        one = get_one()\n\n        # ---- neural -------------------------------------------------------\n        t0 = time.time()\n        spikes, clusters = load_session_spikes(one, eid, probe_rows)\n        timings['load_spikes'] = time.time() - t0\n\n        keep_idx, beryl, n_good = select_neurons(clusters)\n        n_neurons = len(keep_idx)\n        if n_neurons < MIN_NEURONS_PER_SESSION:\n            return {'eid': eid,\n                    'error': f'only {n_neurons} well-isolated grey-matter neurons '\n                             f'(< {MIN_NEURONS_PER_SESSION})'}\n\n        # remap cluster ids of the retained spikes to 0..n_neurons-1\n        remap = np.full(len(clusters), -1, dtype=np.int64)\n        remap[keep_idx] = np.arange(n_neurons)\n        spike_cl = remap[spikes['clusters']]\n        sel = spike_cl >= 0\n        spike_times = np.ascontiguousarray(spikes['times'][sel])\n        spike_cl = np.ascontiguousarray(spike_cl[sel])\n\n        # ---- trials -------------------------------------------------------\n        t0 = time.time()\n        sess_loader, trials, trials_mask = load_trials(one, eid)\n        timings['load_trials'] = time.time() - t0\n        n_trials_raw = len(trials)\n\n        stim_on_all = trials[PARAMS['align_time']].to_numpy()\n        tib_all = trial_number_in_block(trials['probabilityLeft'].to_numpy())\n\n        cand = np.nonzero(trials_mask)[0]\n        if len(cand) == 0:\n            return {'eid': eid, 'error': 'no trials pass the reference trial mask'}\n        interval_begs = stim_on_all[cand] + PARAMS['time_window'][0]\n\n        # ---- continuous behaviours ---------------------------------------\n        t0 = time.time()\n        beh = load_continuous_behaviors(one, eid, sess_loader)\n        timings['load_behavior'] = time.time() - t0\n\n        t0 = time.time()\n        beh_vals, beh_good, beh_reasons = {}, {}, {}\n        for name, d in beh.items():\n            v, g, r = interpolate_behavior(d['times'], d['values'], interval_begs,\n                                           PARAMS['binsize'], N_BINS)\n            beh_vals[name], beh_good[name], beh_reasons[name] = v, g, r\n        timings['bin_behavior'] = time.time() - t0\n\n        keep_trial = np.ones(len(cand), dtype=bool)\n        for name in beh_vals:\n            keep_trial &= beh_good[name]\n        if keep_trial.sum() < 2:\n            return {'eid': eid,\n                    'error': f'only {int(keep_trial.sum())} trials with valid behaviour'}\n\n        trial_idx = cand[keep_trial]\n        interval_begs = interval_begs[keep_trial]\n        n_trials = len(trial_idx)\n\n        # ---- bin spikes ---------------------------------------------------\n        t0 = time.time()\n        binned = bin_spikes(spike_times, spike_cl, n_neurons, interval_begs,\n                            PARAMS['binsize'], N_BINS)\n        timings['bin_spikes'] = time.time() - t0\n\n        # Drop trials in which not a single spike was recorded from the whole\n        # population over the 2 s window. With >= 5 simultaneously recorded\n        # neurons this indicates a dropout in the recording rather than a\n        # genuinely silent population, and such a trial carries no information\n        # for a neural decoder.\n        has_spikes = binned.any(axis=(1, 2))\n        n_zero_spike = int((~has_spikes).sum())\n        if n_zero_spike:\n            binned = binned[has_spikes]\n            trial_idx = trial_idx[has_spikes]\n            interval_begs = interval_begs[has_spikes]\n            keep_trial[np.nonzero(keep_trial)[0][~has_spikes]] = False\n            n_trials = len(trial_idx)\n            if n_trials < 2:\n                return {'eid': eid, 'error': 'fewer than 2 trials with recorded spikes'}\n\n        # ---- inputs -------------------------------------------------------\n        # right edge of each spike bin, relative to stimulus onset\n        bin_times = PARAMS['time_window'][0] + PARAMS['binsize'] * np.arange(1, N_BINS + 1)\n        tib = tib_all[trial_idx].astype(np.float32)\n        inputs = np.empty((n_trials, 2, N_BINS), dtype=np.float32)\n        inputs[:, 0, :] = bin_times[None, :]\n        inputs[:, 1, :] = tib[:, None]\n\n        # ---- outputs ------------------------------------------------------\n        choice = trials['choice'].to_numpy()[trial_idx]\n        # choice == +1 is a LEFT report, choice == -1 a RIGHT report\n        choice_lbl = ((1 - choice) / 2).astype(np.int64)\n\n        pleft = trials['probabilityLeft'].to_numpy()[trial_idx]\n        prior_lbl = np.full(n_trials, -1, dtype=np.int64)\n        for val, lbl in ((0.2, 0), (0.5, 1), (0.8, 2)):\n            prior_lbl[np.isclose(pleft, val)] = lbl\n        if np.any(prior_lbl < 0):\n            bad = np.unique(pleft[prior_lbl < 0])\n            return {'eid': eid, 'error': f'unexpected probabilityLeft values {bad}'}\n\n        ws = beh_vals['wheel-speed'][keep_trial]\n        me = beh_vals['whisker-motion-energy'][keep_trial]\n        ws_lbl, ws_thr = discretize_tertiles(ws)\n        me_lbl, me_thr = discretize_tertiles(me)\n\n        outputs = np.empty((n_trials, 4, N_BINS), dtype=np.int64)\n        outputs[:, 0, :] = choice_lbl[:, None]\n        outputs[:, 1, :] = prior_lbl[:, None]\n        outputs[:, 2, :] = ws_lbl\n        outputs[:, 3, :] = me_lbl\n\n        if show_processing:\n            try:\n                make_processing_figure(\n                    eid, trials, trial_idx, interval_begs, binned, inputs, outputs,\n                    beh, ws, me, ws_thr, me_thr, bin_times, beryl, tib_all, trials_mask)\n            except Exception:\n                traceback.print_exc()\n\n        result = {\n            'eid': eid,\n            'subject': subject,\n            'lab': lab,\n            'neural': [binned[k] for k in range(n_trials)],\n            'input': [inputs[k] for k in range(n_trials)],\n            'output': [outputs[k] for k in range(n_trials)],\n            'beryl': beryl,\n            'info': {\n                'eid': eid,\n                'subject': subject,\n                'lab': lab,\n                'n_probes': int(len(probe_rows)),\n                'n_clusters_total': int(len(clusters)),\n                'n_good_units': n_good,\n                'n_neurons': int(n_neurons),\n                'n_trials_raw': int(n_trials_raw),\n                'n_trials_mask': int(trials_mask.sum()),\n                'n_trials': int(n_trials),\n                'n_zero_spike_trials_dropped': n_zero_spike,\n                # row indices into the session's (unfiltered) trials table for\n                # every retained trial, so the conversion can be re-derived and\n                # checked against the raw ONE objects\n                'trial_idx': trial_idx.astype(np.int32),\n                'whisker_source': beh['whisker-motion-energy']['source'],\n                'wheel_speed_tertiles': ws_thr,\n                'whisker_me_tertiles': me_thr,\n                'mean_firing_rate_hz': float(binned.sum() / (n_neurons * n_trials * 2.0)),\n                'behavior_drop_counts': {\n                    name: int((~beh_good[name]).sum()) for name in beh_good},\n            },\n            'timings': timings,\n            'total_time': time.time() - t_start,\n        }\n        return result\n    except
